318: ### 3.6.5 运行模式与模型：逐场选，且随会话留档
319: 
320: 三种模式（离线回放 / 联网直连 / 联网录制）在**页面里逐场选择**，选择结果写进会话行：`llmMode` 落模式，`meta.model` 落模型名。跑的时候按会话行建客户端——单一事实来源在库里，不需要内存里的映射表，翻回一周前的会话也能说清它当时是怎么跑出来的。
321: 
322: `GET /api/models` 只在显式选择联网后才被前端调用：先 `resolveLiveConfig`，缺 `LLM_BASE_URL` / `LLM_API_KEY` 就**一个网络包都不发**，直接把缺哪个变量告诉界面。拉不到清单不等于不能用——界面是 `input + datalist`，可以手填模型名；`/models` 返回什么也**不是可用性证据**（本机实测它只列出两款模型，而实际能调通四款）。见 D33、D34、F41。

330: 所以第 2 章讲的「意图可解释、可版本化、可 diff」对自建面试官一样成立。

```
### 3.6.8 岗位模板库：12 个行业 × 3 个岗位，点一下把料装好

空白投料台对不懂某个行业的人是死路：不知道该喂什么料，也不知道这工具能问出什么。模板要解决的就是这十秒钟。

模板是**数据不是代码**：36 条 `JobTemplate`（`{id, industry, title, jd, resume, hints, presets, custom}`）放在 `packages/shared/src/templates/`，加一个行业只改数据文件；界面只读，规则不重复实现。三条刻意的选法：**翻库不落库**（`GET /api/templates` 不写任何表，浏览一遍不该往全局面试官库里塞几十位）、**一键采用幂等**（模板自带面试官的 id 由 `customInterviewerId(name, template.id)` 派生，同一个模板永远同一个 id，点两次是复用，返回里的 `created` 让界面说清是哪一种，见 F50）、**每条都必须留可追问的点**（示例简历刻意保留「下降明显」「参与抢救十余次」这类说法并带具体数字，否则一场质询全是「站得住」，恰好演示不出这个产品在干什么）。

字数上下限与推荐阵容进常量 `TEMPLATE_LIMITS`，`templateProblems(list)` 是全库唯一校验入口——测试既拿它验 36 条全过，也拿它验「注入一处毛病会被点名」（F49）。

取材合规：不做招聘平台爬虫（BOSS 直聘 robots.txt 对 `User-agent: *` 写 `Disallow: /*?*`，而职位列表页全带查询参数），36 条模板的 JD 与示例简历全部原创，见 ADR-0011、D42。
```

55: ```bash

64: 3 轮、5 条主张、10 次追问、5 条判定、4 条改写建议会依次出现在界面上，
65: 最后点右上角 **报告 .md / 报告 PDF / 报告 Word** 可以把它带走：Markdown 便于携带，PDF 走浏览器「另存为」（服务端出的是可打印 HTML，不押注你机器上装了什么），Word 可直接接着改。

67: ### 为什么不需要 API Key

71: 这是「过程可复现」的技术底座，也是评委不用配任何东西就能验收的原因。

| D39 | 简历导出**单独一个渲染器**，不复用报告渲染器 | 报告是质询结论（满屏判定徽章、追问实录），简历是要发给 HR 的东西；复用一个文件会让导出的简历带上判定徽章，用户还得自己去删。只复用「怎么出 HTML」（`esc()` 与打印脚本注入），不复用「这一份 HTML 里写什么」；默认导出干净一版，`marks=1` 才标绿并附改动清单；见 ADR-0009、F45 |
--- 181 ---
| D40 | 零门槛入口是「双击一个脚本」，不打包成 exe；判断逻辑全部抽成纯函数 | 打包工具链要本地编译，撞上「十分钟复现」那条硬约束，而且我这台机器打包成功不代表评委机器上双击不被拦；纯函数的原因更实际——「第一次该装依赖、第二次不该」这类逻辑靠人肉点两遍验不出来，抽出来才有 12 条测试守着。桌面快捷方式指向启动脚本而不是 node 本体，且只有真给了图标才写 `IconLocation`；见 ADR-0010 |
--- 182 ---
| D41 | 前端的**提示文案状态必须由纯函数产出**，界面与自动化断言读同一个返回值 | 联网提示的过程态（正在要清单）、缺变量态、报错态、空清单态、没问题态都渲染在同一个 `<p>` 上，而「没问题时元素直接不渲染」——点测脚本靠 `textContent` 判断时，把「元素消失了」误读成「还没好」，于是跑满 18 秒超时后拿上一轮的文字做了断言，PASS 是假的。把状态收进 `packages/web/src/runmode.ts`（`modelNoteOf` / `isPendingNote` / `MODEL_NOTE_PENDING`）之后，有几种态、每种态说什么都成了可枚举、可单测的返回值；见 F48 |
```

</details>

Message bodies (Chinese):

c1:
feat(shared): 36 条原创岗位模板，配一处全库校验

投料台空着的时候，不懂某个行业的人既不知道该喂什么料，也不知道这工具能问出什么。
模板要解决的就是这十秒钟：12 个行业各 3 个岗位，每条自带 JD、示例简历、2–3 条「这类简历常见的坑」
与 2 位推荐面试官 + 1 位该行业的自建面试官草稿。

模板是数据不是代码，所以放在 templates/ 四个分片里，加一个行业只改数据文件。
字数上下限与推荐阵容进 TEMPLATE_LIMITS，templateProblems(list) 是全库唯一校验入口——
浏览模板不发请求、不写库，规则只在服务端与测试各实现一次。

JD 与示例简历全部原创：招聘平台的 robots.txt 明确不许抓（BOSS 直聘对 User-agent: *
写 Disallow: /*?*，而职位列表页全带查询参数），见 AGENTS.md 第 8 节。

c2:
test(shared): 模板的合规、指纹与「只坏一处」的反面断言

36 条全过 templateProblems 只是起点。反面断言必须拿整份 36 条当底子、只注入一处毛病，
否则「总数不对」这一条会把所有规则断言一起兜成通过——第一版就是这么假通过的（F49）。

每条示例简历还必须命中一份模糊措辞表里至少一个词并含数字：一场质询全是「站得住」，
恰好演示不出这个产品在干什么。

c3:
feat(server): 岗位模板库只读浏览与幂等采用两个接口

GET /api/templates 只读：连指纹一起发出去，不写任何表——浏览一遍 36 条模板不该往全局
面试官库里塞几十位面试官。

POST /api/templates/:id/apply 一键采用：把 JD 与示例简历交给前端填进投料台，并把模板自带的
那位面试官装进库里。它的 id 由 customInterviewerId(name, template.id) 派生，同一个模板永远
同一个 id，重复采用落进 ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE，返回 created 让界面说清是「已装好」
还是「复用了」。派生而不是查重：同名判定会被用户改名打破，派生 id 不会（F50）。

c4:
test(server): 覆盖模板形状、翻库不落库与幂等采用

六条：模板形状与指纹；翻库不落库；一键采用后阵容变 3 条且面试官真能上场并 verified；
点两次 created=false、阵容与库里数量都不变；采用后的阵容能建会话；不存在的 id 返回 404。

c5:
feat(web): 投料台的岗位模板选择器，点一下把料装好

行业 chip + 当前行业的 3 个岗位按钮，点一下把岗位名、JD 与示例简历一起填好，
并把返回的阵容整体换上去（模板自带的那位面试官已经在库里了）。

分组、默认行业、失效行业回退与「已装好 / 复用了」的提示文案都放在 templates.ts 的纯函数里，

## 一、P9 验收报告

**做成了什么**：投料台多一排「12 个行业 × 3 个岗位」的模板，点一下把岗位名、JD、示例简历一起填好，并自动把这位面试官装进阵容——不懂某个行业的人也能十秒开工。

![18-templates.png](docs/screenshots/18-templates.png)

**门禁实测**（都是刚跑的，不是估算）

| 项 | 结果 |
| --- | --- |
| `npm run verify` | exit 0 · **45 个测试文件 / 481 条断言 / 4.2 s** + 冒烟 9 项 + vite 37 modules（JS 309.48 kB / gzip 96.40 kB） |
| `npm run e2e:ui` | **76/76**，控制台 0 报错（新增模板场景 9 条：12 行业 / 切行业换岗位 / 点一下三处填好 / 幂等复用 / 清空） |
| `npm run shots` | **18 张**，控制台 0 报错，全部由命令生成 |
| `npm run docs:pdf` | 技术文档 **29 页**（上限 30） |
| 代码规模 | 源码 18047 / 测试 7063（≈0.39） |

**三条刻意的选法**（都写进了 ADR-0011 / D42）：模板是**数据**不是代码（加一个行业只改数据文件）；浏览模板**不写库**（点下去才装面试官）；一键采用**幂等**——面试官 id 从模板 id 派生，同一个模板点两次是「复用」不是「新建」。

**我认为还不完美的地方（不报喜）**

1. **技术文档只剩 1 页余量**（29/30）——这轮又收了一档行高才塞下 3.6.8。后面 P8 还要加内容，得同步删减或再收排版，否则构建会直接失败。
2. **模板覆盖面是 12 个行业**，不是「所有专业」；还剩一个「用户自己传 JD」的场景归 P8 解决。
3. 模板里的示例简历是**合成素材**（这是刻意的：不能拿真人简历当演示料），所以它演示的是「能问出什么」，不是「真实案例库」。
4. 这轮我出过一次误操作：把 709 行的 `findings.md` 写成了空文件，靠 git 一次性还原（已完成，内容无丢失），已记为 F53——写下来比藏起来有用。

**10 个提交**：`fe5a5ef feat(shared)` · `716e3df test(shared)` · `42582ac feat(server)` · `02c8302 test(server)` · `b50552f feat(web)` · `2e19241 test(web)` · `811d676 fix(tools)` · `b8f3de0 fix(docs)` · `18f1ee9 docs` · `ae8ce9d chore(docs)`。仓库 205 → **215 个提交**。

## 二、P8 开工前一页设计评审（请拍板）

**解决谁的什么问题**：模板库解决「不懂这个行业的人不知道该喂什么」。但每个人的目标岗位都不一样，模板只有 36 个。P8 让用户**上传 1–8 份自己的 JD**，系统读完之后按这些要求来审简历——「这个岗位要什么 / 我简历里答得上什么 / 差什么」。

**怎么做**（复用内核，不新造机制）

1. 逐份 JD 抽 `Requirement{ text, anchor(JD 内区间), kind: 硬性/加分/职责 }`——锚定用的是同一个 `resolveAnchor`，**它本来就不管 source 是简历还是 JD**，所以「提问同时锚简历与 JD」不需要新机制，只是多带一个锚点。
2. 跨份聚类合并，记 `sources`；与简历主张对照给 **命中 / 弱命中 / 缺失**。
3. 追问新增两类：命中但站不住 → 用要求原文加压（**双锚点**）；弱命中 → 问边界。**缺失的清单单独成栏，不生成追问**（没写过的经历，逼问没有意义）。

**代价（先说清）**：schema v5 → v6（jd / requirement / requirement_match 三张表）· 新增两个编排阶段（`jd-extract` / `jd-merge`）· **提示词一改，29 盘卡带全部失效必须重录** · 界面从四栏变「左简历右 JD」分屏（roadmap 里已选这个方案）。

**风险**：卡带重录会改写离线演示的每一个数字（截图、文档、点测断言都要跟着更新）；PDF 页数已经只剩 1 页余量。

**怎么验收**：`npm run verify` 全绿 + 新增服务端测试（抽要求 / 聚类 / 三种 match / 双锚点）+ 点测新增「传两份 JD → 看到要求清单与双锚点追问」场景 + 一张 `19-jd.png` 截图 + 卡带重录后离线演示仍能一场跑通。

要我就按这个开工 P8 吗？（也可以先做更轻的 P10「问得更深」，它不碰提示词与卡带——你说顺序，我照办。）

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let's start P8 coding. Big batch. Let me design concretely.

### Data model (schema v6)

Tables:
- `jd` (session_id, id, ordinal, title, text, digest?) — the uploaded JD texts per session
- `requirement` (session_id, id, text, kind, keywords_json, anchor_json, sources_json, created_at) — merged requirements
- `requirement_match` (session_id, requirement_id, claim_id, status, reason, created_at)

Actually simpler: requirement rows per session (post-merge), with `sources` listing jd ids it came from. Match rows link requirement ↔ claim with status (hit/weak/missing).

Shared types:
```ts
export type RequirementKind = 'must' | 'plus' | 'duty';
export const REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL = { must: '硬性要求', plus: '加分项', duty: '岗位职责' };
export interface JobDescription { id: string; title: string; text: string; anchor?: ... }  // hmm anchor of the whole JD?
export interface Requirement {
  id: string;            // R1..Rn deterministic
  text: string;          // 要求原文（可能是合并后的代表句）
  kind: RequirementKind;
  keywords: string[];
  anchor: Anchor;        // 锚在这份 JD 的原文区间（代表句来自哪份 JD）
  jdId: string;
  sources: string[];     // 合并进来的 JD id 列表
}
export type MatchStatus = 'hit' | 'weak' | 'missing';
export const MATCH_LABEL = { hit: '简历里答得上', weak: '答得含糊', missing: '简历里没写' };
export interface RequirementMatch { requirementId: string; claimId: string | null; status: MatchStatus; reason: string; }
export interface DualAnchor { resume: Anchor; jd: Anchor; }  // for probes?
```

For probe dual anchors: `Probe.anchor` is currently the resume anchor. Adding `jdAnchor?: Anchor | null` and maybe `requirementId?: string | null` to Probe. That changes Probe shape → persisted in probe table (anchor_json already; need jd_anchor_json + requirement_id columns).

Prompt changes: probe stage prompt gets a section listing matched requirements with their JD quotes, instructing: for claims that match a requirement with status hit but shaky → cite the requirement text (dual anchor). The model must return `requirementId` and `jdQuote`; we resolve the jdQuote against that JD's text with resolveAnchor. If it fails to resolve → drop the jd anchor but keep probe? Design: if requirementId given but jdQuote unresolvable → we drop the requirement link (probe still valid with resume anchor). If requirementId not in the provided list → ignore.

Extraction stages:
- `jd-extract`: per JD (1..8 calls, parallel), returns list of {text, quote, kind, keywords}. Resolve quote against that JD text via resolveAnchor. Deterministic ids: J1..Jn for jds; R ids assigned after merge: R1..Rn in deterministic order.
- `jd-merge`: merge requirements across JDs into clusters. LLM-based merge (semantic). Deterministic input ordering → deterministic output given same llm outputs. Then optionally match against claims: I could do matching in a separate stage (`jd-match`) per requirement: for each requirement, ask which claims satisfy... That's N calls. Alternative: one call per requirement returning matched claim ids + status + reason. With up to ~20 requirements that's 20 calls. Hmm, budget: it's fine (cassettes grow).

Actually simpler and better for determinism/cost: do match in a `jd-match` stage, one call per requirement, in parallel (like probe). Each returns {claimId|null, status, reason}. Validate claimId exists (else missing/null). Cache/deterministic.

Cassettes: 29 → about 40+ (8 jd-extract + 1 merge + ~10 match + probe changes → probe cassettes all change since prompt changed → re-record everything: extract unchanged? EXTRACT_SYSTEM unchanged probably; probe prompt changes → 9 probe cassettes change. jd stages new. So total recorded cassettes will be re-recorded via scripted-demo.

The scripted demo (`scripted-demo.ts`) must be extended to handle `jd-extract`, `jd-merge`, `jd-match` purposes, and the demo needs JD input. DEMO_JD exists (235 chars single JD). We now support 1-8 JDs. For the demo, keep the single DEMO_JD as one JD (so the demo session uses jd), plus maybe add a second demo JD to showcase merging? Since the demo is the offline showcase and P8's headline feature, the demo should show 2 JDs so merge + missing list are visible. I'll add DEMO_JD_2 (a second related JD, e.g., "后端工程师（Node.js）· 平台架构方向") and set demo to load both.

Hmm, that increases scope. Let me decide: demo loads 2 JDs (both short, original). This showcases: merge across two JDs, sources, missing requirements, dual-anchor probes. Good.

Frontend: split view "左简历右 JD" — add a JD pane (JdBoard) showing: list of uploaded JDs (titles), requirements grouped by kind with match badges, missing requirements list. The user uploads JD via textarea(s) + file import (.txt/.md/.pdf/.docx reuse importer!) — nice reuse: the same import endpoint can parse JD files. Actually `POST /api/import` is generic text extraction; frontend can reuse for JD too.

Session creation: currently `POST /api/sessions` takes resumeText, resumeName, jdText (single), mode, model, interviewers. Change to accept `jds: [{title?, text}]` (1-8) while keeping `jdText` back-compat? AGENTS: minimize churn. I'll support `jds` array and keep `jdText` as a legacy alias (if jds absent and jdText present → one JD titled 目标岗位). meta stores jds.

Run pipeline: before extract (or in parallel), if jds present → jd-extract + merge + match stages, events emitted with new stage names (jd-extract / jd-merge / jd-match) so the UI shows them. Then probe with requirement context; probe stage gets `requirements` + matches for the claims being probed.

Verdict stage: should verdict consider requirement match? Adding requirement to verdict prompt changes verdict cassettes too (already changing probe). Maybe keep verdict unchanged to limit blast radius; the requirement review is its own lane (要求清单 + 命中/弱命中/缺失) and dual-anchor probes feed verdict naturally as evidence. Good — verdict prompt unchanged? But verdict prompt includes probes; probe now may include jd anchor. Does verdict prompt render probes with anchors? `renderProbes` likely shows question only. Let me check: need to read renderProbes/renderInterview implementations to know whether probe text (question + rationale) is passed. If verdict prompt doesn't include jd anchor, verdict cassettes unchanged (but probe questions change → verdict input changes anyway → cassettes change). So all cassettes change regardless.

Budget: requirement-driven probes must respect the same budget: they're probes on claims that matched a requirement. Budget gate counts rounds per claim; requirement-driven probes are still probes on that claim, so no new budget mechanism. Good — design point: "追问预算不用改，因为要求驱动的追问还是挂在主张上".

UI: fifth pane? User approved "左简历右 JD 分屏". Roadmap said interface four columns → "左简历右 JD". Implementation: make the resume pane and JD pane share the fourth column as tabs? "分屏" = split. Let me design: rightmost column becomes two stacked/tab panes: 简历原文 / 岗位要求, with a toggle (data-shot jd-tab / resume-tab). Simpler and bounded: keep four columns; the 4th column ("简历原文") gets a tab switch to "岗位要求" view. That's "左简历右 JD"?? Hmm, actually "左简历右 JD 分屏" means side by side. With four columns already (投料台 / 主张与追问 / 简历原文 / 过程面板), adding a 5th column would be cramped at 1600px. Better: within the right side, the 简历 pane and the JD pane are tabs of the same pane (same width), which keeps layout stable. I'll implement tabs and call it 「简历 / 岗位要求」pane with two tabs. Practical approach: implement 分屏 as a *toggle*: a header switch 「并排看 / 只看一个」 (data-shot='split-toggle'). Default = 分屏 (two half-width panes) on wide screens (CSS grid with min-width media query: ≥1400px split, else tabs?). Hmm complexity.

At 1600px: 投料台 320 + 主张 520 + (简历 260 | 要求 260) + 过程 320 ≈ 1680 — slightly over; panes have internal scroll so it's acceptable. Given the size, plan the work as:

Phase A (shared): types.ts additions in domain.ts (JobDescription/Requirement/RequirementMatch/MatchStatus/labels + Probe.jdAnchor? + requirementId?) + new file requirements.ts with pure helpers: `mergeRequirements` (deterministic clustering by keyword overlap? or LLM-assisted), `matchSummary`, `requirementDigest`, `renderRequirementLine`, `missingRequirements`, `dualAnchorFor`, plus zod schemas for the three new stages (jdList/requirementList/mergeResult/matchResult) in schemas.ts.

Wait: merge is LLM-driven; its output must be validated. So schema `mergeDraft` = { groups: [{ text, kind, sources: number[]/ids, keywords }] }? Let me define carefully:

jd-extract output per JD: `{ requirements: [{ text, quote, kind, keywords: string[] }] }` (quotes resolve against that JD).
jd-merge input: all extracted requirements with ids (R# per jd like JA1, JB2). Output: `{ groups: [{ text, kind, members: string[], keywords }] }` — members = ids of extracted requirements that mean the same thing. Validation in code: members must be known ids and each used at most once (unmatched members fall back to singleton groups, so nothing is lost); text ≤ 120 chars; kind enum. The representative anchor = the member with the longest quote (deterministic).
jd-match input: one requirement + the claim list (like probe) → output `{ claimId: string|null, status: 'hit'|'weak'|'missing', reason }`. Validation: claimId must exist in the provided claims else null→missing; status enum.

Deterministic ids: after merge, requirements get R1..Rn in the order of first appearance (by jd order then extracted order). Groups assigned in that order.

Phase B (server): schema v6 (jd, requirement, requirement_match tables + probe columns jd_anchor_json, requirement_id) + repo functions + orchestrator/{jd.ts} (extractRequirements per JD, mergeRequirements, matchRequirements) + prompts additions (JD_EXTRACT_SYSTEM/buildJdExtractPrompt, JD_MERGE_SYSTEM/buildJdMergePrompt, JD_MATCH_SYSTEM/buildJdMatchPrompt, plus requirement section in probe prompt) + run.ts wiring (new phases producing events) + pipeline (persist + SessionView fields) + http (accept jds, GET /api/sessions/:id includes requirements/matches, maybe GET /api/sessions/:id/requirements).

Phase C (web): api.ts types + fetch; state.ts reducer handles new events; components/JdBoard.tsx; intake JD area (multi-JD add/remove + import); dual-anchor display in ClaimBoard/ResumePane (show JD quote chip next to probe); labels.

Phase D: tests (shared/web/server), scripted-demo extension + seed:demo re-record, e2e scenario + screenshot 19-jd.png, docs (ADR-0012, tech 3.6.9, API table, numbers), commits.

231: function rebuild(db: Db, body: () => void, version: number): void {
232:   db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF');
233:   db.exec('BEGIN');
234:   try {
235:     body();
236:     db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = ' + String(version));

239:     db.exec('ROLLBACK');

241:   } finally {

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Let me see the tail 359-380 quickly? Likely applies DDL then migrations. Fine.

Now, the probe table needs new columns for v6: `requirement_id TEXT`, `jd_quote TEXT`, `jd_start INTEGER`, `jd_end INTEGER`, `jd_exact INTEGER`, `jd_id TEXT`. Since probe table rebuild is needed (SQLite can't add columns via DDL? actually ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN works in SQLite). Simpler: v6 migration uses ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN for the new nullable columns — cheap and avoids rebuild. And new tables jd / requirement / requirement_match. That satisfies "只加表加列不动既有列".

So migrateV6:
```sql
CREATE TABLE jd (...); CREATE TABLE requirement (...); CREATE TABLE requirement_match (...);
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN requirement_id TEXT;
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN jd_id TEXT;
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN jd_quote TEXT;
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN jd_start INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN jd_end INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE probe ADD COLUMN jd_exact INTEGER;
```
And DDL (full) includes those columns in probeTableSql. But careful: probeTableSql is used for both fresh DDL and migration v3 rebuild. If I add the new columns to probeTableSql, then migrateV3 (fresh from v2) would create a probe table with v6 columns and then... migrate V6 would try ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN on an existing column → error. Hmm.

Options: (a) parametrize probeTableSql with `probeJd: boolean` like turnUnique, and DDL uses probeJd:true while DDL_V2/DDL_V3 use false. But DDL_V2 constant and migrateV3 uses probeTableSql(false). So add param `{turnUnique, jdAnchors}`: DDL → {turnUnique:false, jdAnchors:true}; DDL_V2 → {turnUnique:true, jdAnchors:false}; migrateV3 → probeTableSql(false, false). And DDL used by schemaStatements — but schemaStatements is used for both DDL and DDL_V2. So pass options through.

Also: fresh DB creation — does openDatabase exec DDL then set user_version = SCHEMA_VERSION? Let me check db.ts. If a fresh DB is created with full DDL (including v6 columns) and marked version 6, then migrations never run. Good. But if fresh DB is created with DDL and version 0 then migrates 1→6? Let me check db.ts/tail of migrate.

Let me read schema.ts 359-400 and db.ts.

Alternative simpler approach avoiding ALTER: make v6 migration rebuild probe using a v6 probeTableSql with jdAnchors=true and copy old columns (new ones NULL). That's the pattern already used (migrateV3). And parametrize probeTableSql(turnUnique, jdAnchors). Fresh DDL = {false,true}, DDL_V2={true,false}, migrateV3 uses (false,false), migrateV6 rebuilds probe with (false,true).

Rebuild for v6 is a bit more code but consistent with existing patterns and avoids column-vs-migration divergence. I'll do that.

**shared/domain.ts additions**
```ts
export type RequirementKind = 'must' | 'plus' | 'duty';
export const REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL: Record<RequirementKind, string> = { must:'硬性要求', plus:'加分项', duty:'岗位职责' };
export interface JobDescription { id: string; title: string; text: string; anchor: Anchor; }  // anchor = 标题锚? better: JobDescription { id, title, text } (no anchor; anchor belongs to requirement)
export interface Requirement { id: string; kind: RequirementKind; text: string; keywords: string[]; anchor: Anchor; jdId: string; sources: string[]; }
export type MatchStatus = 'hit' | 'weak' | 'missing';
export const MATCH_LABEL: Record<MatchStatus,string> = { hit:'答得上', weak:'答得含糊', missing:'简历里没写' };
export interface RequirementMatch { requirementId: string; claimId: string | null; status: MatchStatus; reason: string; }
```

requirements.ts content:

```ts
/**
 * 岗位要求（多 JD 驱动）的纯函数层。
 *
 * 这里刻意不放任何 LLM 调用：抽取、合并、对照三次模型调用都在服务端编排层，
 * 而这个文件回答的是「拿到模型输出之后，代码怎么把它变成可复现的结构」——
 * 定序、编 id、合并、校验、算摘要。同样的输入必须得到逐字节相同的结果，
 * 否则回放与指纹都会漂移。
 */

import type { Anchor, JobDescription, MatchStatus, Requirement, RequirementKind, RequirementMatch, Claim } from './domain.ts';
import { MATCH_LABEL, REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import { resolveAnchor } from './anchor.ts';
import { shortHash, stableStringify } from './util.ts';

export const REQUIREMENT_LIMITS = {
  maxJds: 8,
  jdMinChars: 40,
  jdMaxChars: 6000,
  totalJdMaxChars: 20000,
  maxRequirementsPerJd: 12,
  maxRequirements: 30,
  minRequirementChars: 4,
  maxRequirementChars: 200,
} as const;

/** 用户在页面上加进来的一份 JD：标题可以不填，代码按顺序兜底命名 */
export function jdTitleOf(raw: string, ordinal: number): string {
  const t = raw.trim();
  return t === '' ? '岗位 ' + String(ordinal) : t.slice(0, 40);
}

export function jdIdOf(ordinal: number): string { return 'J' + String(ordinal); }

export function jdDigest(text: string): string { return shortHash(text.trim(), 16); }

/** 把用户贴进来的 JD 文本规整成入库的样子：统一换行、掐掉首尾空白 */
export function normalizeJdText(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').replace(/[ \t]+$/gm, '').trim();
}

export function jdProblems(jds: readonly {title: string; text: string}[]): string[] { ... }

/** 一条要求在提示词与界面上的短名字 */
export function requirementLabel(r: Requirement): string {
  return REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL[r.kind] + '｜' + (r.text.length > 40 ? r.text.slice(0,40) + '…' : r.text);
}

export interface ExtractedRequirement {
  key: string;      // 'J1#3'
  jdId: string;
  kind: RequirementKind;
  text: string;
  quote: string;
  anchor: Anchor;
  keywords: string[];
}

export interface RejectedRequirement { key: string; jdId: string; quote: string; text: string; reason: string; }

/** 抽取结果的键：jdId + '#' + 该 JD 内的序号。合并阶段模型只认这个键，不认我们自己编的 R 编号 */
export function requirementKey(jdId: string, index: number): string { return jdId + '#' + String(index); }

/** 从模型的一批抽取草稿里挑出能锚定原文的那些（锚不上的逐条记理由丢弃） */
export function groundExtracted(jd: JobDescription, drafts: readonly {...}[], minQuoteLength = 4): { items, rejected }
```
Hmm — the grounding needs the draft shape from zod. shared/requirements.ts shouldn't import schemas types? It can (schemas.ts is in shared). But schemas imports domain only. Fine — I'll define a minimal structural type locally to avoid coupling: `interface RawRequirementDraft { text: string; quote: string; kind: RequirementKind; keywords?: string[] }`.

Then:
```ts
export function groundExtracted(jd, drafts): {items: ExtractedRequirement[], rejected: RejectedRequirement[]}
```
assigning keys J1#1.. in order.

Merge:
```ts
export interface RawGroup { text: string; kind: RequirementKind; members: string[]; keywords?: string[] }
export function mergeRequirements(jds, items, groups): { requirements: Requirement[]; notices: string[]; orphanKeys: string[] }
```
Algorithm:
- byKey map.
- Track used keys.
- For each group in model order: members filtered to known & unused; if empty → skip (record notice). Build Requirement: id R{seq}, kind = raw group kind (validate enum else majority of members), text = group text trimmed (fallback: longest member text), keywords = group keywords (max 6, fallback union of members ≤6), anchor = member with longest quote, jdId = that member's jdId, sources = unique jdIds of members in JD order.
- After groups: ungrouped items → singleton Requirement (notice: '有 N 条要求没有并入任何一组，按独立要求处理').
- Limit total to maxRequirements: keep first N by order, note dropped count.
- Deterministic: same order in → same out.

Also `keywordKey(text)` maybe not needed.

Match helpers:
```ts
export function matchSummary(matches): {hit, weak, missing}
export function matchOf(matches, requirementId): RequirementMatch | null  // or map
export function missingRequirements(requirements, matches): Requirement[]
export function weakRequirements(...)
export function requirementDigest(r): string
export function requirementsForPrompt(requirements, matches, claimIds?: readonly string[]): RequirementNote[]
export interface RequirementNote { id, kind, label, status, claimId, jdQuote, jdTitle }
```
Where jdQuote = r.anchor.quote.

export const MATCH_STATUS_VALUES = ['hit','weak','missing'] as const;
export type SchemaMatchesDomain2 = [ Expect<RequirementKind extends ...>, ...]
export const jdRequirementDraftSchema = z.object({ text: z.string().min(4).max(200), quote: z.string().min(4).max(400), kind: z.enum(REQUIREMENT_KIND_VALUES), keywords: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(24)).max(6).default([]) });
export const jdRequirementListSchema = z.object({ requirements: z.array(jdRequirementDraftSchema).min(1).max(20) });
export const jdGroupDraftSchema = z.object({ text: ..., kind: ..., members: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(24)).min(1).max(40), keywords: ... });
export const jdGroupListSchema = z.object({ groups: z.array(jdGroupDraftSchema).max(40) });
export const jdMatchDraftSchema = z.object({ claimId: z.string().min(1).max(24).nullable().default(null), status: z.enum(MATCH_STATUS_VALUES), reason: z.string().min(1).max(300) });

```
export const JD_EXTRACT_SYSTEM = [
  '你是「岗位要求抽取官」。给你一份招聘 JD，你要挑出**能被拿去追问简历**的要求，逐条列出。',
  '',
  '## 什么算一条要求',
  '- 硬性要求（must）：学历、年限、证书、必须掌握的工具或技能。',
  '- 加分项（plus）：优先考虑、有则更好、某类经验加分。',
  '- 岗位职责（duty）：入职后要负责的具体工作，它同样可以拿来问「你做过吗」。',
  '',
  '## 硬约束（由程序校验，违反的直接丢弃）',
  '1. quote 必须是 JD 原文中**逐字存在**的片段，不少于 4 个字符。不要改写、不要拼接两处不连续的文字。',
  '2. text 是你用一句话复述这条要求，可以改写（例如把「3 年以上」写成「三年以上相关经验」）。',
  '3. 一条要求只写一件事。把「熟悉 Node.js 与 TypeScript」和「有高并发经验」拆成两条。',
  '4. 不要臆造 JD 里没有的要求，也不要把公司福利、薪资、地点写进来。',
  '5. 最多 12 条，挑最能用来检验简历的那些。',
  '',
  '## 输出要求（只输出一个 JSON 对象）',
  '{"requirements":[{"text":"一句话复述的要求","quote":"JD 原文中的逐字片段","kind":"must","keywords":["关键字"]}]}',
  'kind 只能取：must / plus / duty。keywords 最多 6 个，是你认为这条要求里最该被追问的点。',
].join(NL);

export function buildJdExtractPrompt(input: { jd: JobDescription; maxRequirements: number }): string {
  lines: '## 第 N 份 JD：title', '## JD 原文', text, '## 本次最多抽 N 条'
}
```

export const JD_MERGE_SYSTEM = [
  '你是「岗位要求归并官」。你会看到从若干份 JD 里分别抽出来的要求，每条都带一个编号（如 J1#3）。',
  '',
  '## 你的任务',
  '把**说的是同一件事**的要求并成一条，其余的单独成组。',
  '',
  '## 纪律',
  '1. members 只能填下方出现过的编号，不要新造编号；一条编号最多出现在一个组里。',
  '2. 同一个意思换了个说法（「熟悉 TypeScript」与「熟练掌握 TypeScript」）应该并成一组；',
  '   只是同一个行业但要求不同的（「会写 SQL」与「会做数据看板」）不要并。',
  '3. 每组给一句 text 概括这条要求的**最严口径**（两份 JD 一个要 3 年一个要 5 年，就写 5 年）。',
  '4. kind 取组内最强的那个：只要有 must 就是 must；全是 duty 才写 duty。',
  '5. 没有把握并的时候不要硬并——并错一条比多留一条更糟。',
  '6. **不要漏掉任何编号**：每一组列完之后检查一遍，还有哪些编号没被任何组收进去。',
  '',
  '## 输出要求（只输出一个 JSON 对象）',
  '{"groups":[{"text":"概括后的要求","kind":"must","members":["J1#1","J2#2"],"keywords":["关键字"]}]}',
].join(NL);
export function buildJdMergePrompt(items: readonly ExtractedRequirement[]): string

  '',
  '## 三种状态',

].join(NL);

export function buildJdMatchPrompt(input: { requirements: readonly Requirement[]; claims: readonly Claim[] }): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push('## 岗位要求（每条都要对照一次，编号就是 requirementId）');
  for (const r of input.requirements) {
    lines.push('- [' + r.id + '] ' + REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL[r.kind] + '：' + r.text + NL + '  要求原文：' + r.anchor.quote);
  }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('## 这份简历里可以被引用的主张（claimId 只能从这里选）');
  lines.push(renderClaims(input.claims));
  return lines.join(NL);
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 岗位要求三阶段：逐份抽取 → 跨份归并 → 与主张逐条对照。
 * ...
 */
import type {
  Claim, ExtractedRequirement, JobDescription, MatchSummary, MergeResult, RejectedRequirement,
  Requirement, RequirementMatch, RequirementNote,
} from '@crossexam/shared';
import {
  REQUIREMENT_LIMITS, groundExtracted, jdRequirementListSchema, jdGroupListSchema, jdMatchListSchema,
  matchSummary, mergeExtracted, parseLlmOutput, requirementNotes,
} from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { LlmClient, LlmUsage } from '../llm/types.ts';
import { EMPTY_USAGE } from '../llm/types.ts';
import { JD_EXTRACT_SYSTEM, JD_MERGE_SYSTEM, JD_MATCH_SYSTEM, buildJdExtractPrompt, buildJdMergePrompt, buildJdMatchPrompt, stageRequest } from './prompts.ts';

export class JdError extends Error { readonly raw: string; ... }

export interface ExtractRequirementsResult {
  requirements: Requirement[];
  rejected: RejectedRequirement[];
  notices: string[];
  usage: LlmUsage;
  digests: string[];
}

export async function extractRequirements(input: {client; jds: readonly JobDescription[]}): Promise<ExtractRequirementsResult>
```
Implementation: for each JD, one request; parse; groundExtracted. If a JD's parse fails → per-JD failure: record notice '第 N 份 JD 的要求没抽出来：<error>' and continue? Design: 逐条丢弃 vs 整批失败. For JD extraction, per-JD failure should be non-fatal (a bad JD shouldn't kill the run); record notice. That's a meaningful design decision: partial results + explicit notice.

Collect items keyed by jd. Then merge:
```ts
export interface MergeRequirementsResult { merged: MergeResult; usage; digest: string|null; notice? }
export async function mergeRequirements(input: {client; jds; items}): Promise<...>
```
If parse fails → fall back to `mergeExtracted(jds, items, [])` (each item its own requirement) + notice '归并没走通（<err>），每一条要求各自独立处理'. Good fallback.

Then match:
```ts
export interface MatchRequirementsResult {
  matches: RequirementMatch[];
  summary: MatchSummary;
  notes: RequirementNote[];
  usage: LlmUsage;
  notices: string[];
}
export async function matchRequirements(input: {client; jds; requirements; claims}): Promise<MatchRequirementsResult>
```
Implementation: if requirements empty → return empty. One request. Parse fail → all missing with reason '对照没有走通：<err>' + notice.
Validation per requirement (in requirement order): find draft by requirementId; unknown requirementIds ignored (collect count); if not found → `{requirementId, claimId:null, status:'missing', reason:'模型没有对照这一条'}`; if status hit/weak but claimId null or unknown claim → downgrade to 'missing' with reason '模型说能对上，但指不出是哪条主张'? Careful: downgrading is a design decision — better: if claimId unknown/null while status is hit/weak → set status 'weak'? Hmm.     // 岗位要求三阶段只在用户给了 JD 时跑；纯简历场次一个网络包都不多发。

    if (jds.length > 0) {
      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-extract', round: null });
      const pulled = await extractRequirements({ client: input.client, jds });
      usage = addUsage(usage, pulled.usage);
      for (const n of pulled.notices) notices.push(n);
      requirementRejected = pulled.rejected;
      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-merge', round: null });
      const grouped = await mergeRequirements({ client: input.client, jds, items: pulled.items });
      usage = addUsage(usage, grouped.usage);
      for (const n of grouped.notices) notices.push(n);
      requirements = grouped.merged.requirements;
      if (requirements.length === 0) notices.push('没有从岗位要求里抽出任何可对照的条目');
      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-match', round: null });
      const matched = await matchRequirements({ client: input.client, jds, requirements, claims });
      usage = addUsage(usage, matched.usage);
      for (const n of matched.notices) notices.push(n);
      requirementMatches = matched.matches;
      requirementNotes_ = matched.notes;
      await emit({ type: 'requirements', requirements, rejected: requirementRejected, matches: requirementMatches, summary: matched.summary });
    }

- new packages/server/src/orchestrator/jd.ts: extractRequirements({client,jds}) → {items,rejected,notices,usage} (per-JD Promise.all, per-JD parse failure = skip that JD with notice '第 N 份 JD《title》的岗位要求没抽出来：<err>'), mergeRequirements({client,jds,items}) → {merged,notices,usage} (empty items short-circuit; parse failure falls back to mergeExtracted(jds,items,[]) + notice '岗位要求归并没有走通（err），每一条要求各自独立处理'), matchRequirements({client,jds,requirements,claims}) → {matches,summary,notes,notices,usage} (empty short-circuit; parse failure → notice '岗位要求对照没有走通（err），全部按下「简历里没写」处理'; unknown requirementIds counted as stray with notice; hit/weak without a real claimId downgraded to missing with reason '对照说能对上，但指不出是哪条主张，按简历里没写处理'; missing requirements get reason '这一条没有拿到对照结果，按简历里没写处理').

```ts
    // 岗位要求三阶段：只在用户给了 JD 时跑。逐份抽取 → 跨份归并 → 与主张对照。
    // 三条链路都允许部分失败（坏一份 JD、归并没走通、对照没走通），
    // 每次失败都记一条 notice，最坏情况仍然给出一张完整可用的表。
    let requirements: Requirement[] = [];
    let requirementRejected: RejectedRequirement[] = [];
    let requirementMatches: RequirementMatch[] = [];
    let requirementNoteList: RequirementNote[] = [];
    let requirementSummary: MatchSummary = { total: 0, hit: 0, weak: 0, missing: 0 };
    if (jds.length > 0) {
      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-extract', round: null });
      const pulled = await extractRequirements({ client: input.client, jds });
      usage = addUsage(usage, pulled.usage);
      for (const note of pulled.notices) notices.push(note);
      requirementRejected = pulled.rejected;

      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-merge', round: null });
      const grouped = await mergeRequirements({ client: input.client, jds, items: pulled.items });
      usage = addUsage(usage, grouped.usage);
      for (const note of grouped.notices) notices.push(note);
      requirements = grouped.merged.requirements;
      if (requirements.length === 0) notices.push('岗位要求里没有抽出任何可对照的条目');

      await emit({ type: 'stage', stage: 'jd-match', round: null });
      const matched = await matchRequirements({ client: input.client, jds, requirements, claims });
      usage = addUsage(usage, matched.usage);
      for (const note of matched.notices) notices.push(note);
      requirementMatches = matched.matches;
      requirementNoteList = matched.notes;
      requirementSummary = matched.summary;
      await emit({
        type: 'requirements',
        requirements,
        rejected: requirementRejected,
        matches: requirementMatches,
        summary: requirementSummary,
      });
    }
```

Schema v6: three tables:

- `requirement_match` (session_id, requirement_id, claim_id, status, reason) PK (session_id, requirement_id) + FK.

```ts
/* ------------------------------------------------------- 岗位要求（多 JD 驱动） */

interface JdDbRow {
  session_id: string;
  id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  title: string;
  text: string;
}

function mapJd(row: JdDbRow): JobDescription {
  return { id: row.id, ordinal: toInt(row.ordinal), title: row.title, text: row.text };
}

/** JD 原文按顺序写入：ordinal 来自数组下标，J1..Jn 由调用方编好（同一份输入永远同一批编号）。 */
export function insertJds(db: Db, sessionId: string, jds: readonly JobDescription[]): void {
  const stmt = db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO jd (session_id, id, ordinal, title, text) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
  );
  jds.forEach(function (jd, index) {
    stmt.run(sessionId, jd.id, index, jd.title, jd.text);
  });
}

export function listJds(db: Db, sessionId: string): JobDescription[] {
  const rows = asRows<JdDbRow>(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM jd WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal').all(sessionId));
  return rows.map(mapJd);
}

interface RequirementDbRow {
  session_id: string;
  id: string;
  kind: string;
  text: string;
  keywords_json: string;
  jd_id: string;
  sources_json: string;
  quote: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  exact: number;
}

function mapRequirement(row: RequirementDbRow): Requirement {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    kind: row.kind as RequirementKind,
    text: row.text,
    keywords: parseJson<string[]>(row.keywords_json, []),
    anchor: anchorOf(row.quote, row.start, row.end, row.exact),
    jdId: row.jd_id,
    sources: parseJson<string[]>(row.sources_json, []),
  };
}

/** 先清后写：要求清单是一场运行的产物，重跑时不能留下上一轮的 R 编号。 */
export function insertRequirements(db: Db, sessionId: string, requirements: readonly Requirement[]): void {
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM requirement_match WHERE session_id = ?').run(sessionId);
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM requirement WHERE session_id = ?').run(sessionId);
  const stmt = db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO requirement (session_id, id, kind, text, keywords_json, jd_id, sources_json, quote, start, end, exact)' +
      ' VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
  );
  for (const item of requirements) {
    stmt.run(sessionId, item.id, item.kind, item.text, JSON.stringify(item.keywords), item.jdId, JSON.stringify(item.sources), item.anchor.quote, item.anchor.start, item.anchor.end, bit(item.anchor.exact));
  }
}

export function listRequirements(db: Db, sessionId: string): Requirement[] { ... ORDER BY id? }
```
Ordering: ids are R1..Rn; ORDER BY CAST(SUBSTR(id,2) AS INTEGER) — SQLite supports. But requirement ordinal column would be cleaner. Let me add `ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL` to requirement table (index from array) — safer ordering. Yes add ordinal.

Similarly requirement_match: PRIMARY KEY (session_id, requirement_id), FK to requirement(session_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE, claim_id TEXT (nullable, no FK since claim may not exist? it will exist for hit/weak; add FK? composite FK (session_id, claim_id) → claim ON DELETE CASCADE would break for NULL (allowed in SQLite composite FK? NULL in any column makes FK satisfied). Keep it simple: no FK on claim_id to avoid coupling; missing rows have NULL anyway.

Order matches by requirement ordinal via join? Simpler: store `ordinal` in requirement_match too. Hmm, redundant. I'll order matches by requirement_id numeric via SUBSTR. Let me include ordinal in requirement table and join in listRequirementMatches:
`SELECT m.* FROM requirement_match m JOIN requirement r ON r.session_id = m.session_id AND r.id = m.requirement_id WHERE m.session_id = ? ORDER BY r.ordinal`.
Good.

Match row fields: requirement_id, claim_id (nullable), status, reason.

Now write it. Also update `clearRunArtifacts` to delete requirement_match + requirement rows (so rerun doesn't leave stale R rows). jd rows are created at session creation, not cleared.

371|/** 从事件日志里取最后一次某类事件的载荷。事件是真相，表只是索引。 */
372|function lastPayload<T>(events: readonly EventRow[], kind: string, key: string): T[] {
373|  let out: T[] = [];
374|  for (const event of events) {
375|    if (event.kind !== kind) continue;
376|    const value = event.payload[key];
377|    if (Array.isArray(value)) out = value as T[];
378|  }
379|  return out;

Let me define a local helper function `jdsOf(raw, fallbackText)` inside createApp (before routes) or module-level pure function. Module-level is cleaner and testable:

```ts
/**
 * 把请求里的 JD 收成一份清单。
 *
 * 三条刻意的设计：
 *   - 空文本是「还没粘」而不是错误：用户可能正一份份往里贴，先拦下来只会让人困惑；
 *   - 老字段 jdText 仍然认，等价于「只有一份 JD」，老会话与脚本不必改；
 *   - 校验交给 shared 的 jdProblems（份数、长度、重复），错误信息是中文明说缺什么。
 */
function jdsOf(raw: unknown, fallbackText: string): { list: JobDescription[]; problem: string | null } {
  const drafts: { title: string; text: string }[] = [];
  const items = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [];
  for (const item of items) {
    const record = item !== null && typeof item === 'object' ? (item as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    const text = normalizeJdText(textField(record['text']));
    if (text === '') continue;
    drafts.push({ title: textField(record['title']), text });
  }
  if (drafts.length === 0) {
    const legacy = normalizeJdText(fallbackText);
    if (legacy !== '') drafts.push({ title: '', text: legacy });
  }
  const problems = jdProblems(drafts);
  if (problems.length > 0) return { list: [], problem: problems.join(' ') };
  return {
    list: drafts.map((item, index) => ({
      id: jdIdOf(index + 1),
      ordinal: index + 1,
      title: jdTitleOf(item.title, index + 1),
      text: item.text,
    })),
    problem: null,
  };
}
```
Where to place: after helper functions like `knownPanelHint()`. I'll insert before `export function createApp`.

Then meta: `{ source, model: wantedModel, panel: resolved.ids, jdDigests: jds.list.map((jd) => jdDigest(jd.text)) }` and response add `jds: jds.list.map((jd) => ({ id: jd.id, ordinal: jd.ordinal, title: jd.title }))`.
